Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the functional_analysis object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 324. The moduli space has dimension at most 39 with 5 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_324(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 324. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^39(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
